<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 302A. BUSINESS CORPORATIONS</h2>
      <h1 class="fgh">MINNESOTA BUSINESS CORPORATION ACT</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.001">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.001 CITATION.</h1>
        <p>This chapter may be cited as the "Minnesota Business Corporation Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.001.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.125.0">1981 c 270 s 125</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">DEFINITIONS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.011">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.011 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this chapter, unless the language or context clearly indicates that a different meaning is intended, the words, terms, and phrases defined in this section have the meanings given them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/250/#laws.1.45.0">2006 c 250 art 1 s 45</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Address.</span></h2>
          <p>"Address" means mailing address, including a zip code. In the case of a registered office or principal executive office, the term means the mailing address and the actual office location which shall not be a post office box.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Articles.</span></h2>
          <p>"Articles" means, in the case of a corporation incorporated under or governed by this chapter, articles of incorporation, articles of amendment, a resolution of election to become governed by this chapter, a demand retaining the two-thirds majority for shareholder approval of certain transactions, a statement of change of registered office, registered agent, or name of registered agent, a statement establishing or fixing the rights and preferences of a class or series of shares, a statement of cancellation of authorized shares, articles of merger, articles of conversion, articles of abandonment, and articles of dissolution. In the case of a foreign corporation, the term includes all documents serving a similar function required to be filed with the secretary of state or other officer of the foreign corporation's state of incorporation. In the case of a corporation formed under chapter 300, the term means the certificate of incorporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Board.</span></h2>
          <p>"Board" means the board of directors of a corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Class.</span></h2>
          <p>"Class," when used with reference to shares, means a category of shares that differs in designation or one or more rights or preferences from another category of shares of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.6a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6a.<span class="headnote">Closely held corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Closely held corporation" means a corporation which does not have more than 35 shareholders.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Constituent corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Constituent corporation" means a corporation or a foreign corporation that:</p>
          <p>(1) in a merger is either the surviving corporation or a domestic or foreign corporation that is merged into the surviving organization; or</p>
          <p>(2) in an exchange is either the acquiring organization or a corporation whose shares are acquired by the acquiring organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Corporation; domestic corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Corporation" or "domestic corporation" means a corporation, other than a foreign corporation, organized for profit and incorporated under or governed by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Director.</span></h2>
          <p>"Director" means a member of the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Distribution.</span></h2>
          <p>"Distribution" means a direct or indirect transfer of money or other property, other than its own shares, with or without consideration, or an incurrence or issuance of indebtedness, by a corporation to any of its shareholders in respect of its shares. A distribution may be in the form of a dividend or a distribution in liquidation, or as consideration for the purchase, redemption, or other acquisition of its shares, or otherwise.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Filed with the secretary of state.</span></h2>
          <p>"Filed with the secretary of state" means that a document meeting the applicable requirements of this chapter, signed and accompanied by a filing fee of $35, has been delivered to the secretary of state of this state. The secretary of state shall endorse on the document the word "Filed" and the month, day, and year of filing, record the document in the Office of the Secretary of State, and return a document to the person who delivered it for filing.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Foreign corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Foreign corporation" means an organization organized for profit that is incorporated under laws other than the laws of this state for a purpose or purposes for which a corporation may be incorporated under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Good faith.</span></h2>
          <p>"Good faith" means honesty in fact in the conduct of the act or transaction concerned.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Intentionally.</span></h2>
          <p>"Intentionally" means that the person referred to either has a purpose to do or fail to do the act or cause the result specified or believes that the act or failure to act, if successful, will cause that result. A person "intentionally" violates a statute if the person intentionally does the act or causes the result prohibited by the statute, or if the person intentionally fails to do the act or cause the result required by the statute, even though the person may not know of the existence or constitutionality of the statute or the scope or meaning of the terms used in the statute.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Know; knowledge.</span></h2>
          <p>A person "knows" or has "knowledge" of a fact when the person has actual knowledge of it. A person does not "know" or have "knowledge" of a fact merely because the person has reason to know of the fact.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Legal representative.</span></h2>
          <p>"Legal representative" means a person empowered to act for another person, including, but not limited to, an agent, officer, partner, or associate of, an organization; a trustee of a trust; a personal representative; an executor of a will; an administrator of an estate; a trustee in bankruptcy; and a receiver, guardian, custodian, or conservator of a person or a person's estate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Notice.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Notice" is given by a shareholder of a corporation to the corporation or an officer of the corporation when in writing and mailed or delivered to the corporation or the officer at the registered office or principal executive office of the corporation.</p>
          <p>(b) In all other cases, "notice" is given to a person when:</p>
          <p>(1) mailed to the person at an address designated by the person or at the last known address of the person; or</p>
          <p>(2) deposited with a nationally recognized overnight delivery service for overnight delivery or, if overnight delivery to the person is not available, for delivery as promptly as practicable, to the person at an address designated by the person or at the last known address of the person; or</p>
          <p>(3) communicated to the person orally; or</p>
          <p>(4) handed to the person; or</p>
          <p>(5) left at the office of the person with a clerk or other person in charge of the office, or if there is no one in charge, when left in a conspicuous place in the office, or if the office is closed or the person to be notified has no office, when left at the dwelling house or usual place of abode of the person with some person of suitable age and discretion then residing therein.</p>
          <p>(c) Notice is also given by a publicly held corporation to a shareholder if the notice is addressed to the shareholder or group of shareholders in a manner permitted by the rules and regulations under the Securities Exchange Act of 1934, provided that the corporation has first received any affirmative written consent or implied consent required under those rules and regulations.</p>
          <p>(d) Notice by mail is given when deposited in the United States mail with sufficient postage affixed. Notice by deposit for delivery is given when deposited for delivery as provided in paragraph (b), clause (2), after having made sufficient arrangements for payment by the sender.</p>
          <p>(e) Notice is deemed received when it is given.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Officer.</span></h2>
          <p>"Officer" means the chief executive officer, the chief financial officer, a person elected, appointed, or otherwise designated as an officer pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.311">302A.311</a>, and any other person deemed elected as an officer pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.321">302A.321</a>. The term does not include a person elected, appointed, or otherwise designated chair of the board of the corporation, unless otherwise provided in the articles or bylaws.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Organization" means a general partnership, including a limited liability partnership, limited partnership, including a limited liability limited partnership, limited liability company, business trust, corporation, or any other person having a governing statute. The term includes a domestic or foreign organization regardless of whether organized for profit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Outstanding shares.</span></h2>
          <p>"Outstanding shares" means all shares duly issued and not reacquired by a corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Parent.</span></h2>
          <p>"Parent" of a specified corporation means an organization that directly, or indirectly through related organizations, owns more than 50 percent of the voting power of the shares or other ownership interests entitled to vote for directors or other members of the governing body of the specified organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Person.</span></h2>
          <p>"Person" includes a natural person, an organization, and any other association, business trust, estate, trust, enterprise, and any other legal or commercial entity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Principal executive office.</span></h2>
          <p>"Principal executive office" means an office where the elected or appointed chief executive officer of a corporation has an office. If the corporation has no elected or appointed chief executive officer, "principal executive office" means the registered office of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">Registered office.</span></h2>
          <p>"Registered office" means the place in this state designated in the articles of a corporation as the registered office of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.25">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.<span class="headnote">Related organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Related organization" of a specified corporation means:</p>
          <p>(1) a parent or subsidiary of the specified corporation;</p>
          <p>(2) another subsidiary of a parent of the specified corporation;</p>
          <p>(3) a limited liability company owning, directly or indirectly, more than 50 percent of the voting power of the shares entitled to vote for directors of the specified corporation;</p>
          <p>(4) a limited liability company having more than 50 percent of the voting power of its membership interests entitled to vote for members of its governing body owned directly or indirectly by the specified corporation;</p>
          <p>(5) a limited liability company having more than 50 percent of the voting power of its membership interests entitled to vote for members of its governing body owned directly or indirectly either (i) by a parent of the specified corporation or (ii) a limited liability company owning, directly or indirectly, more than 50 percent of the voting power of the shares entitled to vote for directors of the specified corporation; or</p>
          <p>(6) a corporation having more than 50 percent of the voting power of its shares entitled to vote for directors owned directly or indirectly by a limited liability company owning, directly or indirectly, more than 50 percent of the voting power of the shares entitled to vote for directors of the specified corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.26">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.<span class="headnote">Security.</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> "Security" has the meaning given it in section 80A.41(30).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.27">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.<span class="headnote">Series.</span></h2>
          <p>"Series" means a category of shares, within a class of shares authorized or issued by a corporation by or pursuant to its articles, that have some of the same rights and preferences as other shares within the same class, but that differ in designation or one or more rights and preferences from another category of shares within that class.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.28">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 28.<span class="headnote">Share.</span></h2>
          <p>"Share" means one of the units, however designated, into which the shareholders' ownership interests in a corporation are divided.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.29">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29.<span class="headnote">Shareholder.</span></h2>
          <p>"Shareholder" means a person registered on the books or records of a corporation or its transfer agent or registrar as the owner of whole or fractional shares of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.30">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.<span class="headnote">Signed.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Signed" means that the signature of a person has been written on a document, as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/645.44#stat.645.44.14">645.44, subdivision 14</a>, and, with respect to a document required by this chapter to be filed with the secretary of state, means that the document has been signed by a person authorized to do so by this chapter, the articles or bylaws, or a resolution approved by the directors as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.237">302A.237</a> or the shareholders as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.437">302A.437</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A signature on a document may be a facsimile affixed, engraved, printed, placed, stamped with indelible ink, transmitted by facsimile or electronically, or in any other manner reproduced on the document.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.31">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.<span class="headnote">Subsidiary.</span></h2>
          <p>"Subsidiary" of a specified organization means an organization having more than 50 percent of the voting power of its shares or other ownership interests entitled to vote for directors or other members of the governing body of the organization owned directly, or indirectly through related organizations, by the specified organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.32">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.<span class="headnote">Surviving corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Surviving corporation" means the domestic or foreign corporation resulting from a merger.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.33">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/10/#laws.1.33.0">1997 c 10 art 1 s 33</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.34">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.<span class="headnote">Vote.</span></h2>
          <p>"Vote" includes authorization by written action.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.35">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.73.0">1982 c 497 s 73</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.36">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.<span class="headnote">Written action.</span></h2>
          <p>"Written action" means a record signed, or consented to by authenticated electronic communication, by all of the persons required to take the action described. The term also means the counterparts of a record signed, or consented to by authenticated electronic communication, by any of the persons taking the action described. Each counterpart constitutes the action of the persons signing or so consenting to it, and all the counterparts, taken together, constitute one written action by all of the persons signing or so consenting to them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.37">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.<span class="headnote">Acquiring person.</span></h2>
          <p>"Acquiring person" means a person that makes or proposes to make a control share acquisition. When two or more persons act as a partnership, limited partnership, syndicate, or other group pursuant to any written or oral agreement, arrangement, relationship, understanding, or otherwise for the purposes of acquiring, owning, or voting shares of an issuing public corporation, all members of the partnership, syndicate, or other group constitute a "person."</p>
          <p>"Acquiring person" does not include (a) a licensed broker/dealer or licensed underwriter who (1) purchases shares of an issuing public corporation solely for purposes of resale to the public and (2) is not acting in concert with an acquiring person, or (b) a person who becomes entitled to exercise or direct the exercise of a new range of voting power within any of the ranges specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.671#stat.302A.671.2">302A.671, subdivision 2, paragraph (d)</a>, solely as a result of a repurchase of shares by, or recapitalization of, the issuing public corporation or similar action unless (1) the repurchase, recapitalization, or similar action was proposed by or on behalf of, or pursuant to any written or oral agreement, arrangement, relationship, understanding, or otherwise with, the person or any affiliate or associate of the person or (2) the person thereafter acquires beneficial ownership, directly or indirectly, of outstanding shares entitled to vote of the issuing public corporation and, immediately after the acquisition, is entitled to exercise or direct the exercise of the same or a higher range of voting power under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.671#stat.302A.671.2">302A.671, subdivision 2, paragraph (d)</a>, as the person became entitled to exercise as a result of the repurchase, recapitalization, or similar action.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.38">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.<span class="headnote">Control share acquisition.</span></h2>
          <p>"Control share acquisition" means an acquisition, directly or indirectly, by an acquiring person of beneficial ownership of shares of an issuing public corporation that, except for section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.671">302A.671</a>, would, when added to all other shares of the issuing public corporation beneficially owned by the acquiring person, entitle the acquiring person, immediately after the acquisition, to exercise or direct the exercise of a new range of voting power within any of the ranges specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.671#stat.302A.671.2">302A.671, subdivision 2, paragraph (d)</a>, but does not include any of the following:</p>
          <p>(a) an acquisition before, or pursuant to an agreement entered into before, August 1, 1984;</p>
          <p>(b) an acquisition by a donee pursuant to an inter vivos gift not made to avoid section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.671">302A.671</a> or by a distributee as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.1-201">524.1-201, clause (10)</a>;</p>
          <p>(c) an acquisition pursuant to a security agreement not created to avoid section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.671">302A.671</a>;</p>
          <p>(d) an acquisition under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.601">302A.601</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.661">302A.661</a>, if the issuing public corporation is a party to the transaction;</p>
          <p>(e) an acquisition from the issuing public corporation;</p>
          <p>(f) an acquisition for the benefit of others by a person acting in good faith and not made to avoid section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.671">302A.671</a>, to the extent that the person may not exercise or direct the exercise of the voting power or disposition of the shares except upon the instruction of others;</p>
          <p>(g) an acquisition pursuant to a savings, employee stock ownership, or other employee benefit plan of the issuing public corporation or any of its subsidiaries, or by a fiduciary of the plan acting in a fiduciary capacity pursuant to the plan; or</p>
          <p>(h) an acquisition pursuant to an offer to purchase for cash pursuant to a tender offer, or to exchange for stock pursuant to an exchange offer, all shares of the voting stock of the issuing public corporation:</p>
          <p>(1) that has been approved by a majority vote of the members of a committee composed solely of one or more disinterested members of the board of the issuing public corporation formed pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.673#stat.302A.673.1">302A.673, subdivision 1, paragraph (d)</a>, before the commencement of, or the public announcement of the intent to commence, the tender or exchange offer; and</p>
          <p>(2) pursuant to which the acquiring person will become the owner of over 50 percent of the voting stock of the issuing public corporation outstanding at the time of the transaction.</p>
          <p>For purposes of this subdivision, shares beneficially owned by a plan described in clause (g), or by a fiduciary of a plan described in clause (g) pursuant to the plan, are not deemed to be beneficially owned by a person who is a fiduciary of the plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.39">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.<span class="headnote">Issuing public corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Issuing public corporation" means either: (1) a publicly held corporation that has at least 50 shareholders; or (2) any other corporation that has at least 100 shareholders, provided that if, before January 1, 1998, a corporation that has at least 50 shareholders elects to be an issuing public corporation by express amendment contained in the articles or bylaws, including bylaws approved by the board, that corporation is an issuing public corporation if it has at least 50 shareholders.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.40">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Publicly held corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Publicly held corporation" means a corporation that has a class of equity securities registered pursuant to section 12, or is subject to section 15(d), of the Securities Exchange Act of 1934.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.41">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 41.<span class="headnote">Beneficial owner; beneficial ownership.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Beneficial owner," when used with respect to shares or other securities, includes, but is not limited to, any person who, directly or indirectly through any written or oral agreement, arrangement, relationship, understanding, or otherwise, has or shares the power to vote, or direct the voting of, the shares or securities or has or shares the power to dispose of, or direct the disposition of, the shares or securities, except that:</p>
          <p>(1) a person shall not be deemed the beneficial owner of shares or securities tendered pursuant to a tender or exchange offer made by the person or any of the person's affiliates or associates until the tendered shares or securities are accepted for purchase or exchange; and</p>
          <p>(2) a person shall not be deemed the beneficial owner of shares or securities with respect to which the person has the power to vote or direct the voting arising solely from a revocable proxy given in response to a proxy solicitation required to be made and made in accordance with the applicable rules and regulations under the Securities Exchange Act of 1934 and is not then reportable under that act on a Schedule 13D or comparable report, or, if the corporation is not subject to the rules and regulations under the Securities Exchange Act of 1934, would have been required to be made and would not have been reportable if the corporation had been subject to the rules and regulations.</p>
          <p>(b) "Beneficial ownership" includes, but is not limited to, the right to acquire shares or securities through the exercise of options, warrants, or rights, or the conversion of convertible securities, or otherwise. The shares or securities subject to the options, warrants, rights, or conversion privileges held by a person shall be deemed to be outstanding for the purpose of computing the percentage of outstanding shares or securities of the class or series owned by the person, but shall not be deemed to be outstanding for the purpose of computing the percentage of the class or series owned by any other person. A person shall be deemed the beneficial owner of shares and securities beneficially owned by any relative or spouse of the person or any relative of the spouse, residing in the home of the person, any trust or estate in which the person owns ten percent or more of the total beneficial interest or serves as trustee or executor or in a similar fiduciary capacity, any organization in which the person owns ten percent or more of the equity, and any affiliate of the person.</p>
          <p>(c) When two or more persons act or agree to act as a partnership, limited partnership, syndicate, or other group for the purposes of acquiring, owning, or voting shares or other securities of a corporation, all members of the partnership, syndicate, or other group are deemed to constitute a "person" and to have acquired beneficial ownership, as of the date they first so act or agree to act together, of all shares or securities of the corporation beneficially owned by the person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.42">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 42.<span class="headnote">Interested shares.</span></h2>
          <p>"Interested shares" means the shares of an issuing public corporation beneficially owned by any of the following persons: (1) the acquiring person, (2) any officer of the issuing public corporation, or (3) any employee of the issuing public corporation who is also a director of the issuing public corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.43">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 43.<span class="headnote">Affiliate.</span></h2>
          <p>"Affiliate" means a person that directly or indirectly controls, is controlled by, or is under common control with, a specified person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.44">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 44.<span class="headnote">Announcement date.</span></h2>
          <p>"Announcement date," when used in reference to any business combination, means the date of the first public announcement of the final, definitive proposal for the business combination.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.45">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45.<span class="headnote">Associate.</span></h2>
          <p>"Associate," when used to indicate a relationship with any person, means any of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) any organization of which the person is an officer or partner or is, directly or indirectly, the beneficial owner of ten percent or more of any class or series of shares entitled to vote or other equity interest;</p>
          <p>(2) any trust or estate in which the person has a substantial beneficial interest or as to which the person serves as trustee or executor or in a similar fiduciary capacity;</p>
          <p>(3) any relative or spouse of the person, or any relative of the spouse, residing in the home of the person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.46">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 46.<span class="headnote">Business combination.</span></h2>
          <p>"Business combination," when used in reference to any issuing public corporation and any interested shareholder of the issuing public corporation, means any of the following:</p>
          <p>(a) any merger of the issuing public corporation or any subsidiary of the issuing public corporation with (1) the interested shareholder or (2) any other organization (whether or not itself an interested shareholder of the issuing public corporation) that is, or after the merger would be, an affiliate or associate of the interested shareholder, but excluding (i) the merger of a wholly owned subsidiary of the issuing public corporation into the issuing public corporation, (ii) the merger of two or more wholly owned subsidiaries of the issuing public corporation, or (iii) the merger of an organization, other than an interested shareholder or an affiliate or associate of an interested shareholder, with a wholly owned subsidiary of the issuing public corporation pursuant to which the surviving organization, immediately after the merger, becomes a wholly owned subsidiary of the issuing public corporation;</p>
          <p>(b) any exchange, pursuant to a plan of exchange under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.601#stat.302A.601.2">302A.601, subdivision 2</a>, or a comparable statute of any other state or jurisdiction, of shares or other securities of the issuing public corporation or any subsidiary of the issuing corporation or money, or other property for shares, other securities, money, or property of (1) the interested shareholder or (2) any other organization (whether or not itself an interested shareholder of the issuing public corporation) that is, or after the exchange would be, an affiliate or associate of the interested shareholder, but excluding the exchange of shares of a domestic or foreign corporation, other than an interested shareholder or an affiliate or associate of an interested shareholder, pursuant to which the domestic or foreign corporation, immediately after the exchange, becomes a wholly owned subsidiary of the issuing public corporation;</p>
          <p>(c) any sale, lease, exchange, mortgage, pledge, transfer, or other disposition (in a single transaction or a series of transactions), other than sales of goods or services in the ordinary course of business or redemptions pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.671#stat.302A.671.6">302A.671, subdivision 6</a>, to or with the interested shareholder or any affiliate or associate of the interested shareholder, other than to or with the issuing public corporation or a wholly owned subsidiary of the issuing public corporation, of assets of the issuing public corporation or any subsidiary of the issuing public corporation (1) having an aggregate market value equal to ten percent or more of the aggregate market value of all the assets, determined on a consolidated basis, of the issuing public corporation, (2) having an aggregate market value equal to ten percent or more of the aggregate market value of all the outstanding shares of the issuing public corporation, or (3) representing ten percent or more of the earning power or net income, determined on a consolidated basis, of the issuing public corporation except a cash dividend or distribution paid or made pro rata to all shareholders of the issuing public corporation;</p>
          <p>(d) the issuance or transfer by the issuing public corporation or any subsidiary of the issuing public corporation (in a single transaction or a series of transactions) of any shares of, or other ownership interests in, the issuing public corporation or any subsidiary of the issuing public corporation that have an aggregate market value equal to five percent or more of the aggregate market value of all the outstanding shares of the issuing public corporation to the interested shareholder or any affiliate or associate of the interested shareholder, except pursuant to the exercise of warrants or rights to purchase shares offered, or a dividend or distribution paid or made, pro rata to all shareholders of the issuing public corporation other than for the purpose, directly or indirectly, of facilitating or effecting a subsequent transaction that would have been a business combination if the dividend or distribution had not been made;</p>
          <p>(e) the adoption of any plan or proposal for the liquidation or dissolution of the issuing public corporation, or any reincorporation of the issuing public corporation in another state or jurisdiction, proposed by or on behalf of, or pursuant to any written or oral agreement, arrangement, relationship, understanding, or otherwise with, the interested shareholder or any affiliate or associate of the interested shareholder;</p>
          <p>(f) any reclassification of securities (including without limitation any share dividend or split, reverse share split, or other distribution of shares in respect of shares), recapitalization of the issuing public corporation, merger of the issuing public corporation with any subsidiary of the issuing public corporation, exchange of shares of the issuing public corporation with any subsidiary of the issuing public corporation, or other transaction (whether or not with or into or otherwise involving the interested shareholder), proposed by or on behalf of, or pursuant to any written or oral agreement, arrangement, relationship, understanding, or otherwise with, the interested shareholder or any affiliate or associate of the interested shareholder, that has the effect, directly or indirectly, of increasing the proportionate share of the outstanding shares of any class or series of shares entitled to vote, or securities that are exchangeable for, convertible into, or carry a right to acquire shares entitled to vote, of the issuing public corporation or any subsidiary of the issuing public corporation that is, directly or indirectly, owned by the interested shareholder or any affiliate or associate of the interested shareholder, except as a result of immaterial changes due to fractional share adjustments;</p>
          <p>(g) any receipt by the interested shareholder or any affiliate or associate of the interested shareholder of the benefit, directly or indirectly (except proportionately as a shareholder of the issuing public corporation), of any loans, advances, guarantees, pledges, or other financial assistance, or any tax credits or other tax advantages provided by or through the issuing public corporation or any subsidiary of the issuing public corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.47">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 47.<span class="headnote">Consummation date.</span></h2>
          <p>"Consummation date," with respect to any business combination, means the date of consummation of the business combination or, in the case of a business combination as to which a shareholder vote is taken, the later of (1) the business day before the vote or (2) 20 days before the date of consummation of the business combination.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.48">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48.<span class="headnote">Control.</span></h2>
          <p>"Control," including the terms "controlling," "controlled by," and "under common control with," means the possession, directly or indirectly, of the power to direct or cause the direction of the management and policies of a person, whether through the ownership of voting securities, by contract, or otherwise. A person's beneficial ownership of ten percent or more of the voting power of a corporation's outstanding shares entitled to vote in the election of directors creates a presumption that the person has control of the corporation. Notwithstanding the foregoing, a person is not considered to have control of a corporation if the person holds voting power, in good faith and not for the purpose of avoiding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.673">302A.673</a>, as an agent, bank, broker, nominee, custodian, or trustee for one or more beneficial owners who do not individually or as a group have control of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.49">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 49.<span class="headnote">Interested shareholder.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Interested shareholder," when used in reference to any issuing public corporation, means any person that is (1) the beneficial owner, directly or indirectly, of ten percent or more of the voting power of the outstanding shares entitled to vote of the issuing public corporation or (2) an affiliate or associate of the issuing public corporation that, at any time within the four-year period immediately before the date in question, was the beneficial owner, directly or indirectly, of ten percent or more of the voting power of the then outstanding shares entitled to vote of the issuing public corporation.</p>
          <p>(b) If a person who has not been a beneficial owner of ten percent or more of the voting power of the outstanding shares entitled to vote of the issuing public corporation immediately prior to a repurchase of shares by, or recapitalization of, the issuing public corporation or similar action shall become a beneficial owner of ten percent or more of the voting power solely as a result of the share repurchase, recapitalization, or similar action, the person shall not be deemed to be the beneficial owner of ten percent or more of the voting power for purposes of paragraph (a), clause (1) or (2), unless:</p>
          <p>(1) the repurchase, recapitalization, conversion, or similar action was proposed by or on behalf of, or pursuant to any agreement, arrangement, relationship, understanding, or otherwise (whether or not in writing) with, the person or any affiliate or associate of the person; or</p>
          <p>(2) the person thereafter acquires beneficial ownership, directly or indirectly, of outstanding shares entitled to vote of the issuing public corporation and, immediately after the acquisition, is the beneficial owner, directly or indirectly, of ten percent or more of the voting power of the outstanding shares entitled to vote of the issuing public corporation.</p>
          <p>(c) Interested shareholder does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) the issuing public corporation or any of its subsidiaries;</p>
          <p>(2) a savings, employee stock ownership, or other employee benefit plan of the issuing public corporation or its subsidiary, or a fiduciary of the plan when acting in a fiduciary capacity pursuant to the plan; or</p>
          <p>(3) a licensed broker/dealer or licensed underwriter who:</p>
          <p>(i) purchases shares of an issuing public corporation solely for purposes of resale to the public; and</p>
          <p>(ii) is not acting in concert with an interested shareholder.</p>
          <p>(d) For purposes of this subdivision, shares beneficially owned by a plan described in paragraph (c), clause (2), or by a fiduciary of a plan described in paragraph (c), clause (2), pursuant to the plan, are not deemed to be beneficially owned by a person who is a fiduciary of the plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.50">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 50.<span class="headnote">Market value.</span></h2>
          <p>"Market value," when used in reference to shares or other property of any corporation, means the following:</p>
          <p>(a) In the case of shares, the average closing sale price of a share during the 30 trading days immediately preceding the date in question or, with respect to the references in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.553#stat.302A.553.3">302A.553, subdivision 3</a>, if a person or persons selling the shares have commenced a tender offer or have announced an intention to seek control of the corporation, during the 30 trading days preceding the earlier of the commencement of the tender offer or the making of the announcement, in either case:</p>
          <p>(1) on the composite tape for New York Stock Exchange listed shares; or</p>
          <p>(2) if the shares are not quoted on the composite tape or not listed on the New York Stock Exchange, on the principal United States securities exchange registered under the Securities Exchange Act of 1934, which may include the NASDAQ Stock Market, on which the shares are listed; or</p>
          <p>(3) if the shares are not listed on any such exchange, on any system then in use.</p>
          <p>If no quotation under clauses (1) through (3) is available, then the market value is the fair market value on the date in question of the shares as determined in good faith by the board of the corporation.</p>
          <p>(b) In the case of property other than cash or shares, the fair market value of the property on the date in question as determined in good faith by the board of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.51">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 51.<span class="headnote">Share acquisition date.</span></h2>
          <p>"Share acquisition date," with respect to any person and any issuing public corporation, means the date that the person first becomes an interested shareholder of the issuing public corporation. Notwithstanding the foregoing provisions of this subdivision:</p>
          <p>(a) if a person becomes, on one or more dates, an interested shareholder of the issuing public corporation, but thereafter ceases to be an interested shareholder of the issuing public corporation, and subsequently again becomes an interested shareholder, "share acquisition date," with respect to that person means the date on which the person most recently became an interested shareholder of the issuing public corporation; and</p>
          <p>(b) if, on or after August 1, 2004, a person is the beneficial owner, directly or indirectly, of ten percent or more of the voting power of the outstanding shares entitled to vote of the issuing public corporation at the time the issuing public corporation becomes a publicly held corporation, "share acquisition date," with respect to that person means the date on which the person first became the beneficial owner, directly or indirectly, of ten percent or more of the voting power of the outstanding shares entitled to vote of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.52">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 52.<span class="headnote">Offeror.</span></h2>
          <p>"Offeror" means a person who makes or in any way participates in making a takeover offer. Offeror does not include a bank or broker-dealer loaning funds to an offeror in the ordinary course of its business or a bank, broker-dealer, attorney, accountant, consultant, employee, or other person furnishing information or advice to or performing ministerial duties for an offeror and not otherwise participating in the takeover offer. When two or more persons act as a partnership, limited partnership, syndicate, or other group pursuant to any agreement, arrangement, relationship, understanding, or otherwise, whether or not in writing, for the purpose of acquiring, owning, or voting shares of a target company, all members of the partnership, syndicate, or other group constitute "a person."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.53">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 53.<span class="headnote">Takeover offer.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Takeover offer" means an offer to acquire shares of an issuing public corporation from a shareholder pursuant to a tender offer or request or invitation for tenders, if, after the acquisition of all shares acquired pursuant to the offer:</p>
          <p>(1) the offeror would be directly or indirectly a beneficial owner of more than ten percent of any class or series of the outstanding shares of the issuing public corporation and was directly or indirectly the beneficial owner of ten percent or less of that class or series of the outstanding shares of the issuing public corporation before commencement of the offer; or</p>
          <p>(2) the beneficial ownership by the offeror of any class or series of the outstanding shares of the issuing public corporation would be increased by more than ten percent of that class or series and the offeror was directly or indirectly the beneficial owner of ten percent or more of any class or series of the outstanding shares of the issuing public corporation before commencement of the offer.</p>
          <p>(b) Takeover offer does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) an offer in connection with the acquisition of a share which, together with all other acquisitions by the offeror of shares of the same class or series of shares of the issuer, would not result in the offeror having acquired more than two percent of that class or series during the preceding 12-month period;</p>
          <p>(2) an offer by the issuer to acquire its own shares unless the offer is made during the pendency of a takeover offer by a person who is not an associate or affiliate of the issuer;</p>
          <p>(3) an offer in which the issuing public corporation is an insurance company subject to regulation by the commissioner of commerce, a financial institution regulated by the commissioner, or a public service utility subject to regulation by the public utilities commission.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.54">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 54.<span class="headnote">Division or combination.</span></h2>
          <p>"Division" or "combination" means dividing or combining shares of a class or series, whether issued or unissued, into a greater or lesser number of shares of the same class or series.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.55">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 55.<span class="headnote">Acquiring organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Acquiring organization" means a corporation, foreign corporation, or domestic or foreign limited liability company that acquires in an exchange the shares of a corporation or foreign corporation or the membership interests of a domestic or foreign limited liability company.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.56">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 56.<span class="headnote">Constituent organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Constituent organization" means a corporation, foreign corporation, limited liability company or foreign limited liability company that:</p>
          <p>(1) in a merger is either the surviving organization or an organization that is merged into the surviving organization; or</p>
          <p>(2) in an exchange is either the acquiring organization or an organization whose securities are acquired by the acquiring organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.57">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 57.<span class="headnote">Owners.</span></h2>
          <p>"Owners" means shareholders in the case of a corporation or foreign corporation and members in the case of a limited liability company.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.58">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 58.<span class="headnote">Ownership interests.</span></h2>
          <p>"Ownership interests" means shares in the case of a corporation or foreign corporation, membership interests in the case of a limited liability company, and governance or transferable interests in the case of any other organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.59">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 59.<span class="headnote">Surviving organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Surviving organization" means the corporation or foreign corporation or domestic or foreign limited liability company resulting from a merger.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.60">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 60.<span class="headnote">Electronic communication.</span></h2>
          <p>"Electronic communication" means any form of communication, not directly involving the physical transmission of paper, that creates a record that may be retained, retrieved, and reviewed by a recipient of the communication, and that may be directly reproduced in paper form by the recipient through an automated process.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.61">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 61.<span class="headnote">Remote communication.</span></h2>
          <p>"Remote communication" means communication via electronic communication, conference telephone, video conference, the Internet, or such other means by which persons not physically present in the same location may communicate with each other on a substantially simultaneous basis.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.62">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 62.<span class="headnote">Authenticated.</span></h2>
          <p>"Authenticated" means, with respect to an electronic communication, that the communication is delivered to the principal place of business of the corporation, or to an officer or agent of the corporation authorized by the corporation to receive the communication, and that the communication sets forth information from which the corporation can reasonably conclude that the communication was sent by the purported sender.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.63">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 63.<span class="headnote">Converted organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Converted organization" means the organization into which a converting organization converts pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.682">302A.682</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.692">302A.692</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.64">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 64.<span class="headnote">Converting organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Converting organization" means an organization that converts into another organization pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.682">302A.682</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.692">302A.692</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.65">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 65.<span class="headnote">Governing body.</span></h2>
          <p>"Governing body" means the body of an organization selected by its owners that has the ultimate power to determine the organization's policies and control its activities. The governing body of a domestic corporation is its board of directors, and the governing body of a domestic limited liability company is its board of governors.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.66">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 66.<span class="headnote">Limited liability company.</span></h2>
          <p>"Limited liability company" means either a domestic or a foreign limited liability company, unless specified otherwise in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.67">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 67.<span class="headnote">Converting corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Converting corporation" means a converting organization that is a corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.68">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 68.<span class="headnote">Organizational documents.</span></h2>
          <p>"Organizational documents" means:</p>
          <p>(1) for a domestic or foreign general partnership, its partnership agreement;</p>
          <p>(2) for a limited partnership or foreign limited partnership, its certificate of limited partnership and partnership agreement;</p>
          <p>(3) for a domestic or foreign limited liability company, its certificate or articles of organization and operating agreement, or comparable documents as provided in its governing statute;</p>
          <p>(4) for a business trust, its agreement of trust and declaration of trust;</p>
          <p>(5) for a domestic or foreign corporation for profit, its articles of incorporation, bylaws, and other agreements among its shareholders that are authorized by its governing statute, or comparable documents as provided in its governing statute; and</p>
          <p>(6) for any other organization, the basic records that create the organization and determine its internal governance and the relations among the persons that own it, have an interest in it, or are members of it, in each case as provided or authorized by its governing statute.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.69">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 69.<span class="headnote">Personal liability.</span></h2>
          <p>"Personal liability" means liability for a debt, obligation, or other liability of an organization that is imposed on a person that co-owns, has an interest in, or is a member of the organization:</p>
          <p>(1) by the governing statute solely by reason of the person co-owning, having an interest in, or being a member of the organization; or</p>
          <p>(2) by the organization's organizational documents under a provision of the governing statute authorizing those documents to make one or more specified persons liable for all or specified debts, obligations, or other liabilities of the organization solely by reason of the person or persons co-owning, having an interest in, or being a member of the organization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.70">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 70.<span class="headnote">Governing statute.</span></h2>
          <p>"Governing statute" means the statute that governs an organization's internal affairs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.011.71">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 71.<span class="headnote">Record.</span></h2>
          <p>"Record" means information that is inscribed on a tangible medium or that is stored in an electronic or other medium and is retrievable in perceivable form.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/103/#laws.0.1.0">2018 c 103 s 1</a>,2</p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">LEGAL RECOGNITION OF ELECTRONIC<br>RECORDS AND SIGNATURES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.015">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.015 LEGAL RECOGNITION OF ELECTRONIC RECORDS AND SIGNATURES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.015.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the words, terms, and phrases defined in this subdivision have the meanings given them.</p>
          <p>(b) "Electronic" means relating to technology having electrical, digital, magnetic, wireless, optical, electromagnetic, or similar capabilities.</p>
          <p>(c) "Electronic record" means a record created, generated, sent, communicated, received, or stored by electronic means.</p>
          <p>(d) "Electronic signature" means an electronic sound, symbol, or process attached to or logically associated with a record and executed or adopted by a person with the intent to sign the record.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.015.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Electronic records and signatures.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this chapter:</p>
          <p>(1) a record or signature may not be denied legal effect or enforceability solely because it is in electronic form;</p>
          <p>(2) a contract may not be denied legal effect or enforceability solely because an electronic record was used in its formation;</p>
          <p>(3) if a provision requires a record to be in writing, an electronic record satisfies the requirement; and</p>
          <p>(4) if a provision requires a signature, an electronic signature satisfies the requirement.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.015.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/311/#laws.1.9.0">2002 c 311 art 1 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/103/#laws.0.3.0">2018 c 103 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">APPLICATION</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.021">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.021 APPLICATION AND ELECTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Election by chapter 300 corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation incorporated under chapter 300 that has not subsequently become governed by chapter 301 and that was incorporated for a purpose or purposes for which a corporation may be incorporated under this chapter may elect to become governed by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Election by business and professional corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation incorporated under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/301.01">301.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/301.67">301.67</a> may elect, on or after July 1, 1981 and before January 1, 1984, to become governed by this chapter. A corporation incorporated under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/301.01">301.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/301.67">301.67</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/319A.01">319A.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/319A.22">319A.22</a> may elect, on or after July 1, 1981 and before January 1, 1984, to become governed by this chapter and sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/319A.01">319A.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/319A.22">319A.22</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Conforming articles of electing corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>If the articles of an electing corporation include a provision prohibited by this chapter or omit a provision required by this chapter or are otherwise inconsistent with this chapter, the electing corporation shall amend its articles to conform to the requirements of this chapter. The appropriate provisions of the corporation's articles or bylaws or the law by which it was governed before the effective date of the election made pursuant to this section control the manner of adoption of the amendment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Method of election.</span></h2>
          <p>An election by a corporation to become governed by this chapter shall be made by resolution approved by the affirmative vote of the holders of the same proportion or number of the voting power of the shares entitled to vote that is required for amendment of the articles of the corporation prior to the election. The resolution, and articles of amendment if required, shall be filed with the secretary of state and is effective upon filing. If no amendment of the articles is required, the resolution shall state that the articles of the corporation conform to the requirements of this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Effect of election upon bylaws.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon filing an election pursuant to subdivision 4, all provisions of the bylaws that are consistent with this chapter remain or become effective and all provisions of the bylaws that are inconsistent with this chapter cease to be effective.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Choice of incorporation until January 1, 1984.</span></h2>
          <p>From July 1, 1981 to December 31, 1983, inclusive, a corporation incorporated for a purpose or purposes for which a corporation may be incorporated under this chapter may be incorporated either under this chapter or under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/301.01">301.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/301.67">301.67</a>, or, if applicable, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/301.01">301.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/301.67">301.67</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/319A.01">319A.01</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/319A.22">319A.22</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Nonelecting business corporations subject to law as of January 1, 1984.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation in existence on January 1, 1984 and incorporated under another statute of this state for a purpose or purposes for which a corporation may be incorporated under this chapter or, if applicable, this chapter and chapter 319A, other than a corporation incorporated under chapter 300 that has not subsequently become governed by chapter 301, that has not elected before January 1, 1984 to become subject to this chapter, becomes governed by this chapter or, if applicable, this chapter and chapter 319A, on January 1, 1984 as fully as though the corporation had been incorporated under this chapter or, if applicable, this chapter and chapter 319A. All provisions of the articles and bylaws of the corporation that may be included in the articles or bylaws under this chapter remain in effect. All provisions of the articles and bylaws of the corporation that are inconsistent with this chapter cease to be effective on January 1, 1984. Any provisions required by this chapter to be contained in the articles that do not appear in the articles are read into them as a matter of law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.7a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Chapter 300 corporation subject to law as of August 1, 2006.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation incorporated under chapter 300 in existence on August 1, 2006, becomes governed by this chapter on August 1, 2006, as fully as though the corporation had been incorporated under this chapter, except as specifically otherwise provided by law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Retention of two-thirds majority.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the articles of a corporation described in subdivision 1 or 2 and electing to become governed by this chapter or, if applicable, this chapter and chapter 319A, or described in subdivision 7, do not contain a provision specifying the proportion of the voting power of the shares required for approval of amendments to the articles, plans of merger or exchange, or sales of assets, a shareholder or shareholders holding more than one-third of the voting power of all the shares entitled to vote for any or all of the above mentioned actions may, by signed written demand filed with the secretary of state, amend the articles of the corporation to include a provision requiring the approval of the holders of two-thirds of the voting power of the shares entitled to vote for any or all of the above mentioned actions for which no required majority was specified, notwithstanding any provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.135">302A.135</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.613">302A.613</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.661">302A.661</a> to the contrary. Notice that the demand has been filed shall be given by the shareholder to an officer of the corporation, but failure to give the notice does not invalidate the demand.</p>
          <p>(b) A shareholder or shareholders holding more than one-third of the voting power of the shares entitled to vote for dissolution of a corporation described in subdivision 1 or 2 and electing to become governed by this chapter or, if applicable, this chapter and chapter 319A, or described in subdivision 7, may, by signed written demand filed with the secretary of state, amend the articles of the corporation to include a provision requiring the approval of the holders of two-thirds of the voting power of the shares for the authorization of the dissolution of the corporation, notwithstanding the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.721">302A.721</a>. Notice that the demand has been filed shall be given by the shareholder to an officer of the corporation, but failure to give the notice does not invalidate the demand.</p>
          <p>(c) A signed written demand by the shareholders of a corporation pursuant to paragraph (a) or (b) is valid only if filed with the secretary of state before January 1, 1984 or, in the case of a corporation described in subdivision 7, before April 1, 1984.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Incorporation after January 1, 1984.</span></h2>
          <p>Effective January 1, 1984, a corporation incorporated for a purpose or purposes for which a corporation may be incorporated under this chapter shall be incorporated only under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.021.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Laws not to apply.</span></h2>
          <p> Chapters 301, 316, and 556 do not apply to a corporation incorporated under or governed by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.021.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.2.0">1981 c 270 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.10.0">1982 c 497 s 10</a>-13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/69/#laws.1.17.0">2005 c 69 art 1 s 17</a>,18; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/227/#laws.2.16.0">2014 c 227 art 2 s 16</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.031">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.031 TRANSITION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.031.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Continuation of legal acts.</span></h2>
          <p>The continuation or completion of any act by a corporation that has not incorporated under, but has become governed by, this chapter, and the continuation or performance of any executed or wholly or partially executory contract, conveyance, or transfer to or by the corporation, shall, if otherwise lawful before the corporation became governed by this chapter, remain valid, and may be continued, completed, consummated, enforced, or terminated as required or permitted by a statute applicable prior to the date on which the corporation became governed by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.031.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Transition of preemptive rights.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of denial of preemptive rights under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.413#stat.302A.413.1">302A.413, subdivision 1</a>, the articles of a corporation formed under chapter 301 shall be construed to deny completely preemptive rights for all shares, rights to purchase shares, securities other than shares or rights to purchase securities other than shares, if those articles deny shareholders the preemptive right to purchase or subscribe to shares.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.031.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Perpetual duration granted for chapter 300 corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) All corporations formed under chapter 300 and governed by this chapter pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.021#stat.302A.021.7a">302A.021, subdivision 7a</a>, are granted perpetual duration irrespective of the period of duration set forth in their articles of incorporation. This grant may be modified in the articles as authorized under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.111#stat.302A.111.2">302A.111, subdivision 2, paragraph (b)</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) All corporations formed under chapter 300 and governed by this chapter pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/47.13">47.13</a>, are granted perpetual duration irrespective of the period of duration set forth in their certificates of incorporation. This grant may be modified in the certificate of incorporation as authorized under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/47.12#stat.47.12.2">47.12, subdivision 2, paragraph (a), clause (3)</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.031.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.3.0">1981 c 270 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/618/#laws.0.10.0">1984 c 618 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/69/#laws.1.19.0">2005 c 69 art 1 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.041">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.041 RESERVATION OF RIGHT.</h1>
        <p>The state reserves the right to amend or repeal the provisions of this chapter. A corporation incorporated under or governed by this chapter is subject to this reserved right.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.041.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.4.0">1981 c 270 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">INCORPORATION; ARTICLES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.101">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.101 PURPOSES.</h1>
        <p>A corporation may be incorporated under this chapter for any business purpose or purposes, unless some other statute of this state requires incorporation for any of those purposes under a different law. Unless otherwise provided in its articles, a corporation has general business purposes.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.101.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.5.0">1981 c 270 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.105">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.105 INCORPORATORS.</h1>
        <p>One or more natural persons of at least 18 years of age may act as incorporators of a corporation by filing with the secretary of state articles of incorporation for the corporation.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.105.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.6.0">1981 c 270 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.5.0">1993 c 17 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.111">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.111 ARTICLES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.111.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Required provisions.</span></h2>
          <p>The articles of incorporation shall contain:</p>
          <p>(a) the name of the corporation;</p>
          <p>(b) the address of the registered office of the corporation and the name of its registered agent, if any, at that address;</p>
          <p>(c) the aggregate number of shares that the corporation has authority to issue; and</p>
          <p>(d) the name and address of each incorporator.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.111.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Statutory provisions that may be modified only in articles or in a shareholder control agreement.</span></h2>
          <p>The following provisions govern a corporation unless modified in the articles or in a shareholder control agreement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.457">302A.457</a>:</p>
          <p>(a) a corporation has general business purposes (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.101">302A.101</a>);</p>
          <p>(b) a corporation has perpetual existence and certain powers (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.161">302A.161</a>);</p>
          <p>(c) the power to adopt, amend, or repeal the bylaws is vested in the board (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.181">302A.181</a>);</p>
          <p>(d) a corporation must allow cumulative voting for directors (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.215#stat.302A.215.2">302A.215, subdivision 2</a>);</p>
          <p>(e) the affirmative vote of a majority of directors present is required for an action of the board (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.237">302A.237</a>);</p>
          <p>(f) a written action by the board taken without a meeting must be signed by all directors (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.239">302A.239</a>);</p>
          <p>(g) the board may authorize the issuance of securities and rights to purchase securities (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.401#stat.302A.401.1">302A.401, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(h) all shares are common shares entitled to vote and are of one class and one series (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.401#stat.302A.401.2">302A.401, subdivision 2</a>, clauses (a) and (b));</p>
          <p>(i) all shares have equal rights and preferences in all matters not otherwise provided for by the board (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.401#stat.302A.401.2">302A.401, subdivision 2</a>, clause (b));</p>
          <p>(j) the par value of shares is fixed at one cent per share for certain purposes and may be fixed by the board for certain other purposes (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.401#stat.302A.401.2">302A.401, subdivision 2</a>, clause (c));</p>
