          <p>(k) the board or the shareholders may issue shares for any consideration or for no consideration to effectuate share dividends, divisions, or combinations, and determine the value of nonmonetary consideration (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.405#stat.302A.405.1">302A.405, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(l) shares of a class or series must not be issued to holders of shares of another class or series to effectuate share dividends, divisions, or combinations, unless authorized by a majority of the voting power of the shares of the same class or series as the shares to be issued (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.405#stat.302A.405.1">302A.405, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(m) a corporation may issue rights to purchase securities whose terms, provisions, and conditions are fixed by the board (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.409">302A.409</a>);</p>
          <p>(n) a shareholder has certain preemptive rights, unless otherwise provided by the board (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.413">302A.413</a>);</p>
          <p>(o) the affirmative vote of the holders of a majority of the voting power of the shares present and entitled to vote at a duly held meeting is required for an action of the shareholders, except where this chapter requires the affirmative vote of a plurality of the votes cast (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.215#stat.302A.215.1">302A.215, subdivision 1</a>) or a majority of the voting power of all shares entitled to vote (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.437#stat.302A.437.1">302A.437, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(p) shares of a corporation acquired by the corporation may be reissued (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.553#stat.302A.553.1">302A.553, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(q) each share has one vote unless otherwise provided in the terms of the share (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.445#stat.302A.445.3">302A.445, subdivision 3</a>);</p>
          <p>(r) a corporation may issue shares for a consideration less than the par value, if any, of the shares (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.405#stat.302A.405.2">302A.405, subdivision 2</a>);</p>
          <p>(s) the board may effect share dividends, divisions, and combinations under certain circumstances without shareholder approval (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.402">302A.402</a>);</p>
          <p>(t) a written action of shareholders must be signed by all shareholders (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.441">302A.441</a>);</p>
          <p>(u) specified amendments of the articles create dissenters' rights (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.471">302A.471</a>, subdivision 1, clause (a)); and</p>
          <p>(v) shareholders are entitled to vote as a class or series upon proposed amendments to the articles in specified circumstances (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.137">302A.137</a>).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.111.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Statutory provisions that may be modified in articles, in a shareholder control agreement, or in bylaws.</span></h2>
          <p>The following provisions govern a corporation unless modified in the articles, in a shareholder control agreement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.457">302A.457</a>, or in the bylaws:</p>
          <p>(a) directors serve for an indefinite term that expires at the next regular meeting of shareholders (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.207">302A.207</a>);</p>
          <p>(b) the compensation of directors is fixed by the board (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.211">302A.211</a>);</p>
          <p>(c) a certain method must be used for removal of directors (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.223">302A.223</a>);</p>
          <p>(d) a certain method must be used for filling board vacancies (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.225">302A.225</a>);</p>
          <p>(e) if the board fails to select a place for a board meeting, it must be held at the principal executive office (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.231#stat.302A.231.1">302A.231, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(f) the notice of a board meeting need not state the purpose of the meeting (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.231#stat.302A.231.3">302A.231, subdivision 3</a>);</p>
          <p>(g) a majority of the board is a quorum for a board meeting (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.235">302A.235</a>);</p>
          <p>(h) a committee shall consist of one or more persons, who need not be directors, appointed by affirmative vote of a majority of the directors present (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.241#stat.302A.241.2">302A.241, subdivision 2</a>), and a committee may create one or more subcommittees, each consisting of one or more members of the committee, and may delegate to a subcommittee any or all of the authority of the committee (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.241#stat.302A.241.2a">302A.241, subdivision 2a</a>);</p>
          <p>(i) the board may establish a special litigation committee (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.241">302A.241</a>);</p>
          <p>(j) the chief executive officer and chief financial officer have specified duties, until the board determines otherwise (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.305">302A.305</a>);</p>
          <p>(k) officers may delegate some or all of their duties and powers, if not prohibited by the board from doing so (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.351">302A.351</a>);</p>
          <p>(l) the corporation may establish uncertificated shares (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.417#stat.302A.417.7">302A.417, subdivision 7</a>);</p>
          <p>(m) regular meetings of shareholders need not be held, unless demanded by a shareholder under certain conditions (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.431">302A.431</a>);</p>
          <p>(n) in all instances where a specific minimum notice period has not otherwise been fixed by law, not less than ten-days notice is required for a meeting of shareholders (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.435#stat.302A.435.2">302A.435, subdivision 2</a>);</p>
          <p>(o) the number of shares required for a quorum at a shareholders' meeting is a majority of the voting power of the shares entitled to vote at the meeting (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.443">302A.443</a>);</p>
          <p>(p) the board may fix a date up to 60 days before the date of a shareholders' meeting as the date for the determination of the holders of shares entitled to notice of and entitled to vote at the meeting (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.445#stat.302A.445.1">302A.445, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(q) indemnification of certain persons is required (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.521">302A.521</a>); and</p>
          <p>(r) the board may authorize, and the corporation may make, distributions not prohibited, limited, or restricted by an agreement (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.551#stat.302A.551.1">302A.551, subdivision 1</a>).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.111.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Optional provisions; specific subjects.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions in paragraphs (a), (g), (q), (r), and (u) may be included in the articles.</p>
          <p>The provisions in paragraphs (b) to (f), (h) to (p), (s), and (t) may be included either in the articles or the bylaws:</p>
          <p>(a) the members of the first board may be named in the articles (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.201#stat.302A.201.1">302A.201, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(b) a manner for increasing or decreasing the number of directors may be provided (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.203">302A.203</a>);</p>
          <p>(c) additional qualifications for directors may be imposed (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.205">302A.205</a>);</p>
          <p>(d) directors may be classified (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.213">302A.213</a>);</p>
          <p>(e) the day or date, time, and place of board meetings may be fixed (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.231#stat.302A.231.1">302A.231, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(f) absent directors may be permitted to give written consent or opposition to a proposal (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.233">302A.233</a>);</p>
          <p>(g) a larger than majority vote may be required for board action (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.237">302A.237</a>);</p>
          <p>(h) authority to sign and deliver certain documents may be delegated to an officer or agent of the corporation other than the chief executive officer (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.305#stat.302A.305.2">302A.305, subdivision 2</a>);</p>
          <p>(i) additional officers may be designated (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.311">302A.311</a>);</p>
          <p>(j) additional powers, rights, duties, and responsibilities may be given to officers (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.311">302A.311</a>);</p>
          <p>(k) a method for filling vacant offices may be specified (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.341#stat.302A.341.3">302A.341, subdivision 3</a>);</p>
          <p>(l) a certain officer or agent may be authorized to sign share certificates (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.417#stat.302A.417.2">302A.417, subdivision 2</a>);</p>
          <p>(m) the transfer or registration of transfer of securities may be restricted (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.429">302A.429</a>);</p>
          <p>(n) the day or date, time, and place of regular shareholder meetings may be fixed (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.431#stat.302A.431.3">302A.431, subdivision 3</a>);</p>
          <p>(o) certain persons may be authorized to call special meetings of shareholders (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.433#stat.302A.433.1">302A.433, subdivision 1</a>);</p>
          <p>(p) notices of shareholder meetings may be required to contain certain information (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.435#stat.302A.435.3">302A.435, subdivision 3</a>);</p>
          <p>(q) a larger than majority vote may be required for shareholder action (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.437">302A.437</a>);</p>
          <p>(r) voting rights may be granted in or pursuant to the articles to persons who are not shareholders (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.445#stat.302A.445.4">302A.445, subdivision 4</a>);</p>
          <p>(s) corporate actions giving rise to dissenter rights may be designated (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.471#stat.302A.471.1">302A.471, subdivision 1</a>, clause (g));</p>
          <p>(t) the rights and priorities of persons to receive distributions may be established (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.551">302A.551</a>); and</p>
          <p>(u) a director's personal liability to the corporation or its shareholders for monetary damages for breach of fiduciary duty as a director may be eliminated or limited in the articles (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.251#stat.302A.251.4">302A.251, subdivision 4</a>).</p>
          <p>Nothing in this subdivision limits the right of the board, by resolution, to take an action that may be included in the bylaws under this subdivision without including it in the bylaws, unless it is required to be included in the bylaws by another provision of this chapter. Nothing in this subdivision limits the permissible scope of a shareholder control agreement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.457">302A.457</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.111.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Optional provisions: generally.</span></h2>
          <p>The articles may contain other provisions not inconsistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.201">302A.201</a> or any other provision of law relating to the management of the business or the regulation of the affairs of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.111.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Powers need not be stated.</span></h2>
          <p>It is not necessary to set forth in the articles any of the corporate powers granted by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.111.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Dependence on facts outside of the articles.</span></h2>
          <p>Except for provisions included pursuant to subdivision 1, any provision of the articles may:</p>
          <p>(a) be made dependent upon facts ascertainable outside the articles, but only if the manner in which the facts operate upon the provision is clearly and expressly set forth in the articles; and</p>
          <p>(b) incorporate by reference some or all of the terms of any agreements, contracts, or other arrangements entered into by the corporation, but only if the corporation retains at its principal executive office a copy of the agreements, contracts, or other arrangements or the portions incorporated by reference.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.111.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.7.0">1981 c 270 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.14.0">1982 c 497 s 14</a>-16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/618/#laws.0.11.0">1984 c 618 s 11</a>,12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/2/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/104/#laws.0.7.0">1987 c 104 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/203/#laws.0.1.0">1987 c 203 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/172/#laws.0.3.0">1989 c 172 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/49/#laws.0.1.0">1991 c 49 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.6.0">1993 c 17 s 6</a>,7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/10/#laws.1.7.0">1997 c 10 art 1 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/85/#laws.1.3.0">1999 c 85 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/199/#laws.14.7.0">2004 c 199 art 14 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/250/#laws.1.15.0">2006 c 250 art 1 s 15</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/233/#laws.1.3.0">2008 c 233 art 1 s 3</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/170/#laws.0.6.0">2014 c 170 s 6</a>,7</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.115">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.115 CORPORATE NAME.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.115.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements; prohibitions.</span></h2>
          <p>The corporate name:</p>
          <p>(a) Shall be in the English language or in any other language expressed in English letters or characters;</p>
          <p>(b) Shall contain the word "corporation," "incorporated," or "limited," or shall contain an abbreviation of one or more of these words, or the word "company" or the abbreviation "Co." if that word or abbreviation is not immediately preceded by the word "and" or the character "&amp;";</p>
          <p>(c) Shall not contain a word or phrase that indicates or implies that it is incorporated for a purpose other than a legal business purpose;</p>
          <p>(d) Shall be distinguishable upon the records in the Office of the Secretary of State from the name of each domestic corporation, limited partnership, limited liability partnership, and limited liability company, whether profit or nonprofit, and each foreign corporation, limited partnership, limited liability partnership, and limited liability company on file, authorized or registered to do business in this state at the time of filing, whether profit or nonprofit, and each name the right to which is, at the time of incorporation, reserved as provided for in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5.35">5.35</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.117">302A.117</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/321.0109">321.0109</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/322C.0109">322C.0109</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a>, unless there is filed with the articles one of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) The written consent of the domestic corporation, limited partnership, limited liability partnership, or limited liability company, or the foreign corporation, limited partnership, limited liability partnership, or limited liability company authorized or registered to do business in this state or the holder of a reserved name or a name filed by or registered with the secretary of state under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a> having a name that is not distinguishable;</p>
          <p>(2) A certified copy of a final decree of a court in this state establishing the prior right of the applicant to the use of the name in this state; or</p>
          <p>(3) The applicant's affidavit that the domestic or foreign corporation, limited partnership, or limited liability company with the name that is not distinguishable has been incorporated or on file in this state for at least three years prior to the affidavit, if it is a domestic corporation, limited partnership, or limited liability company, or has been authorized or registered to do business in this state for at least three years prior to the affidavit, if it is a foreign corporation, limited partnership, or limited liability company, or that the holder of a name filed or registered with the secretary of state under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a> filed or registered that name at least three years prior to the affidavit; that the domestic or foreign corporation, limited partnership, or limited liability company or holder has not during the three-year period before the affidavit filed any document with the secretary of state; that the applicant has mailed written notice to the domestic or foreign corporation, limited partnership, or limited liability company or the holder of a name filed or registered with the secretary of state under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a> by certified mail, return receipt requested, properly addressed to the registered office of the domestic or foreign corporation or limited liability company or in care of the agent of the limited partnership, or the address of the holder of a name filed or registered with the secretary of state under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a>, shown in the records of the secretary of state, stating that the applicant intends to use a name that is not distinguishable and the notice has been returned to the applicant as undeliverable to the addressee domestic or foreign corporation, limited partnership, limited liability company, or holder of a name filed or registered with the secretary of state under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a>; that the applicant, after diligent inquiry, has been unable to find any telephone listing for the domestic or foreign corporation, limited partnership, or limited liability company with the name that is not distinguishable in the county in which is located the registered office of the domestic or foreign corporation, limited partnership, or limited liability company shown in the records of the secretary of state or has been unable to find any telephone listing for the holder of a name filed or registered with the secretary of state under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a> in the county in which is located the address of the holder shown in the records of the secretary of state; and that the applicant has no knowledge that the domestic or foreign corporation, limited partnership, limited liability company, or holder of a name filed or registered with the secretary of state under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a> is currently engaged in business in this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.115.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Names continued.</span></h2>
          <p>Subdivision 1, clause (d) does not affect the right of a domestic corporation existing on January 1, 1984, or a foreign corporation authorized to do business in this state on that date to continue the use of its name.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.115.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Determination.</span></h2>
          <p>The secretary of state shall determine whether a name is "distinguishable" from another name for purposes of this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.117">302A.117</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.115.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Other laws affecting use of names.</span></h2>
          <p>This section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.117">302A.117</a> do not abrogate or limit the law of unfair competition or unfair practices, nor sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a>, nor the laws of the United States with respect to the right to acquire and protect copyrights, trade names, trademarks, service names, service marks, or any other rights to the exclusive use of names or symbols, nor derogate the common law or the principles of equity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.115.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Use of name by successor corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>A domestic or foreign corporation that is the surviving organization in a merger with one or more other organizations, or that is incorporated by the reorganization of one or more organizations, or that acquires by sale, lease, or other disposition to or exchange with an organization all or substantially all of the assets of another organization, including its name, may have the same name as that used in this state by any of the other organizations, if the other organization whose name is sought to be used was organized under the laws of, or is authorized to transact business in, this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.115.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Injunction.</span></h2>
          <p>The use of a name by a corporation in violation of this section does not affect or vitiate its corporate existence, but a court in this state may, upon application of the state or of a person interested or affected, enjoin the corporation from doing business under a name assumed in violation of this section, although its articles may have been filed with the secretary of state and a certificate of incorporation issued.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.115.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Lost names; use by others.</span></h2>
          <p>Each corporation formed before July 1, 1979, which has not filed the active status report required by Minnesota Statutes 1982, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/301.511">301.511</a> or an annual registration under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.821">302A.821</a> and which has not elected to become governed by this chapter before January 1, 1984, shall file the annual registration under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.821">302A.821</a>.</p>
          <p>Each corporation which has not filed that report or registration loses its right to the exclusive use of its name. The corporation may reacquire the right to use that name by filing the registration under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.821">302A.821</a>, unless the name has been adopted for use or reserved by another person, in which case the registration will be rejected unless the registration is accompanied by a consent, court order, or affidavit pursuant to subdivision 1, clause (d). A corporation which cannot reacquire the use of its corporate name shall adopt a new corporate name which complies with the provisions of this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.115.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/203/#laws.0.14.0">2008 c 203 s 14</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.115.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.8.0">1981 c 270 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.17.0">1982 c 497 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/368/#laws.0.3.0">1983 c 368 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/618/#laws.0.13.0">1984 c 618 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/655/#laws.1.56.0">1984 c 655 art 1 s 56</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/682/#laws.0.7.0">1988 c 682 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/292/#laws.0.3.0">1989 c 292 s 3</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/517/#laws.1.13.0">1992 c 517 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.8.0">1993 c 17 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/128/#laws.2.1.0">1995 c 128 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/10/#laws.1.8.0">1997 c 10 art 1 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/311/#laws.1.10.0">2002 c 311 art 1 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/199/#laws.13.110.0">2004 c 199 art 13 s 110</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/250/#laws.1.17.0">2006 c 250 art 1 s 17</a>,18; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/98/#laws.0.8.0">2009 c 98 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/157/#laws.2.9.0">2014 c 157 art 2 s 9</a>,29,31</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.117">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.117 RESERVED NAME.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.117.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Who may reserve.</span></h2>
          <p>The exclusive right to the use of a corporate name otherwise permitted by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.115">302A.115</a> may be reserved by:</p>
          <p>(a) a person doing business in this state under that name;</p>
          <p>(b) a person intending to incorporate under this chapter;</p>
          <p>(c) a domestic corporation intending to change its name;</p>
          <p>(d) a foreign corporation intending to make application for a certificate of authority to transact business in this state;</p>
          <p>(e) a foreign corporation authorized to transact business in this state and intending to change its name;</p>
          <p>(f) a person intending to incorporate a foreign corporation and intending to have the foreign corporation make application for a certificate of authority to transact business in this state; or</p>
          <p>(g) a foreign corporation doing business under that name or a name not distinguishable from that name in one or more states other than this state and not described in clause (d), (e), or (f).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.117.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Method of reservation.</span></h2>
          <p>The reservation shall be made by filing with the secretary of state a request that the name be reserved. If the name is available for use by the applicant, the secretary of state shall reserve the name for the exclusive use of the applicant for a period of 12 months. The reservation may be renewed for successive 12-month periods.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.117.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Transfer of reservation.</span></h2>
          <p>The right to the exclusive use of a corporate name reserved pursuant to this section may be transferred to another person by or on behalf of the applicant for whom the name was reserved by filing with the secretary of state a notice of the transfer and specifying the name and address of the transferee.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.117.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.9.0">1981 c 270 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/292/#laws.0.6.0">1989 c 292 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.9.0">1993 c 17 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.302A.121"><b>302A.121</b> MS 2008 [Subds 1 and 2 repealed by amendment, 2010 c 250 art 2 s 8]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.121">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.121 REGISTERED OFFICE; REGISTERED AGENT.</h1>
        <p>Every corporation shall have a registered office, and may have a registered agent, in the manner prescribed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5.36">5.36</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.121.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.10.0">1981 c 270 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/517/#laws.1.14.0">1992 c 517 art 1 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/128/#laws.3.2.0">1995 c 128 art 3 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/250/#laws.2.8.0">2010 c 250 art 2 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.302A.123"><b>302A.123</b> MS 2008 [Subds 1 to 3 repealed by amendment, 2010 c 250 art 2 s 9]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.123">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.123 CHANGE OF REGISTERED OFFICE OR REGISTERED AGENT; CHANGE OF NAME OF REGISTERED AGENT.</h1>
        <p>Every corporation may change its registered office or change its registered agent, and the agent may resign, or change its business address or name, in the manner prescribed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5.36">5.36</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.123.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.11.0">1981 c 270 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.18.0">1982 c 497 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/236/#laws.0.4.0">1989 c 236 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.10.0">1993 c 17 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/250/#laws.2.9.0">2010 c 250 art 2 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.131">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.131 AMENDMENT OF ARTICLES.</h1>
        <p>The articles of a corporation may be amended at any time to include or modify any provision that is required or permitted to appear in the articles or to omit any provision not required to be included in the articles, except that when articles are amended to restate them, the name and address of each incorporator may be omitted. Unless otherwise provided in this chapter, the articles may be amended or modified only in accordance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.133">302A.133</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.139">302A.139</a>. An amendment which merely restates the then-existing articles of incorporation, as amended, is not an amendment for the purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.215#stat.302A.215.2">302A.215, subdivision 2</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.413#stat.302A.413.9">302A.413, subdivision 9</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.131.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.12.0">1981 c 270 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.19.0">1982 c 497 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/618/#laws.0.14.0">1984 c 618 s 14</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.133">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.133 PROCEDURE FOR AMENDMENT WHEN NO SHARES ARE OUTSTANDING.</h1>
        <p>Before the issuance of shares by a corporation, the articles may be amended pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.171">302A.171</a> by the incorporators or by the board. The articles may also be amended by the board to change or cancel a statement pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.401#stat.302A.401.3">302A.401, subdivision 3</a>, establishing or fixing the rights and preferences of a class or series of shares before the issuance of any shares of that class or series or at any subsequent time that no shares of that class or series are outstanding by filing articles of amendment or a statement of cancellation, as appropriate, with the secretary of state. If a statement filed pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.401#stat.302A.401.3">302A.401, subdivision 3</a>, is canceled, the shares of the class and series originally covered by the statement have the status of authorized but unissued, undesignated shares, unless the articles otherwise provide. If the articles provide that the canceled shares may not be reissued, the statement of cancellation must include the information specified by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.553#stat.302A.553.2">302A.553, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.133.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.13.0">1981 c 270 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/104/#laws.0.9.0">1987 c 104 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.11.0">1993 c 17 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.135">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.135 PROCEDURE FOR AMENDMENT AFTER ISSUANCE OF SHARES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.135.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Manner of amendment.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as otherwise set forth in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.133">302A.133</a>, after the issuance of shares by the corporation, the articles may be amended in the manner set forth in this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.135.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Submission to shareholders.</span></h2>
          <p>A resolution approved by the affirmative vote of a majority of the directors present, or proposed by a shareholder or shareholders holding three percent or more of the voting power of the shares entitled to vote, that sets forth the proposed amendment shall be submitted to a vote at the next regular or special meeting of the shareholders of which notice has not yet been given but still can be timely given. Any number of amendments may be submitted to the shareholders and voted upon at one meeting, but the same or substantially the same amendment proposed by a shareholder or shareholders need not be submitted to the shareholders or be voted upon at more than one meeting during a 15-month period, except that if a corporation is registered or reporting under the federal securities laws, the provisions of this sentence do not apply to the extent that those provisions are in conflict with the federal securities laws or rules adopted under those laws. The resolution may amend the articles in their entirety to restate and supersede the original articles and all amendments to them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.135.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Notice.</span></h2>
          <p>Written notice of the shareholders' meeting setting forth the substance of the proposed amendment shall be given to each shareholder entitled to vote in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.435">302A.435</a> for the giving of notice of meetings of shareholders.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.135.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Approval by shareholders.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The proposed amendment is adopted when approved by the affirmative vote of the shareholders required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.437">302A.437</a>, except as provided in paragraphs (b) and (c) and subdivision 5.</p>
          <p>(b) For a closely held corporation, if the articles provide for a specified proportion or number equal to or larger than the majority necessary to transact a specified type of business at a meeting, or if it is proposed to amend the articles to provide for a specified proportion or number equal to or larger than the majority necessary to transact a specified type of business at a meeting, the affirmative vote necessary to add the provision to, or to amend an existing provision in, the articles is the larger of:</p>
          <p>(1) the specified proportion or number or, in the absence of a specific provision, the affirmative vote necessary to transact the type of business described in the proposed amendment at a meeting immediately before the effectiveness of the proposed amendment; or</p>
          <p>(2) the specified proportion or number that would, upon effectiveness of the proposed amendment, be necessary to transact the specified type of business at a meeting.</p>
          <p>(c) For corporations other than closely held corporations, if the articles provide for a larger proportion or number to transact a specified type of business at a meeting, the affirmative vote of that larger proportion or number is necessary to amend the articles to decrease the proportion or number necessary to transact the business.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.135.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Certain restatements.</span></h2>
          <p>An amendment that merely restates the existing articles, as amended, may be authorized by a resolution approved by the board and may, but need not, be submitted to and approved by the shareholders as provided in subdivisions 2, 3, and 4.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.135.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Investment companies.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any contrary provision of this chapter, the board of directors of a corporation that is registered as an open-end management investment company under the Investment Company Act of 1940 may, without shareholder approval, increase or decrease, but not below the then-outstanding shares, the aggregate number of shares the corporation has authority to issue, including shares of any class or series, unless a provision has been included in the corporation's articles prohibiting the board from increasing or decreasing the aggregate number of shares, or any class or series of shares, as applicable, that the corporation has authority to issue.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.135.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Change of corporate name.</span></h2>
          <p>An amendment that only changes a corporation's corporate name may be authorized by a resolution approved by the board and may, but need not, be submitted to and approved by the shareholders as provided in subdivisions 2, 3, and 4.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.135.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.14.0">1981 c 270 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.20.0">1982 c 497 s 20</a>,21; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/5/#laws.0.1.0">1985 c 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/104/#laws.0.10.0">1987 c 104 s 10</a>,11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.12.0">1993 c 17 s 12</a>,13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/417/#laws.0.1.0">1994 c 417 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/264/#laws.0.1.0">2000 c 264 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/311/#laws.1.11.0">2002 c 311 art 1 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/250/#laws.1.19.0">2006 c 250 art 1 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.137">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.137 CLASS OR SERIES VOTING ON AMENDMENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.137.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Amendments creating rights.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in subdivision 2, the holders of the outstanding shares of a class or series are entitled to vote as a class or series upon a proposed amendment, whether or not entitled to vote thereon by the provisions of the articles, if the amendment would:</p>
          <p>(a) effect an exchange, reclassification, or cancellation of all or part of the shares of the class or series, or effect a combination of outstanding shares of a class or series into a lesser number of shares of the class or series where each other class and series is not subject to a similar combination;</p>
          <p>(b) effect an exchange, or create a right of exchange, of all or any part of the shares of another class or series for the shares of the class or series;</p>
          <p>(c) change the rights or preferences of the shares of the class or series;</p>
          <p>(d) create a new class or series of shares having rights and preferences prior and superior to the shares of that class or series, or increase the rights and preferences or the number of authorized shares, of a class or series having rights and preferences prior or superior to the shares of that class or series;</p>
          <p>(e) divide the shares of the class into series and determine the designation of each series and the variations in the relative rights and preferences between the shares of each series, or authorize the board to do so;</p>
          <p>(f) limit or deny any existing preemptive rights of the shares of the class or series; or</p>
          <p>(g) cancel or otherwise affect distributions on the shares of the class or series that have accrued but have not been declared.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.137.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Combined voting groups.</span></h2>
          <p>The articles may provide that, if a proposed amendment entitling the holders of the outstanding shares of two or more classes or series to vote as separate classes or series under subdivision 1 would affect those classes or series in the same or a substantially similar way, the holders of the outstanding shares of all the classes or series so affected must vote together as a single voting group on the proposed amendment.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.137.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.15.0">1981 c 270 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/104/#laws.0.12.0">1987 c 104 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.14.0">1993 c 17 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/199/#laws.14.8.0">2004 c 199 art 14 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/170/#laws.0.8.0">2014 c 170 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.139">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.139 ARTICLES OF AMENDMENT.</h1>
        <p>When an amendment has been adopted, articles of amendment shall be prepared that contain:</p>
        <p>(a) the name of the corporation;</p>
        <p>(b) the amendment adopted;</p>
        <p>(c) with respect to an amendment restating the articles, a statement that the amendment restating the articles correctly sets forth without change the corresponding provisions of the articles as previously amended if the amendment was approved only by the board;</p>
        <p>(d) if the amendment provides for but does not establish the manner for effecting an exchange, reclassification, division, combination, or cancellation of issued shares, a statement of the manner in which it will be effected; and</p>
        <p>(e) a statement that the amendment has been adopted pursuant to this chapter.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.139.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.16.0">1981 c 270 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/104/#laws.0.13.0">1987 c 104 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/682/#laws.0.9.0">1988 c 682 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/49/#laws.0.2.0">1991 c 49 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.141">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.141 EFFECT OF AMENDMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.141.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Effect on cause of action.</span></h2>
          <p>An amendment does not affect an existing cause of action in favor of or against the corporation, nor a pending suit to which the corporation is a party, nor the existing rights of persons other than shareholders.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.141.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Effect of change of name.</span></h2>
          <p>If the corporate name is changed by the amendment, a suit brought by or against the corporation under its former name does not abate for that reason.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.141.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Effect of amendments restating articles.</span></h2>
          <p>When effective under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.153">302A.153</a>, an amendment restating the articles in their entirety supersedes the original articles and all amendments to the original articles.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.141.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.17.0">1981 c 270 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/104/#laws.0.14.0">1987 c 104 s 14</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.151">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.151 FILING ARTICLES.</h1>
        <p>Unless filed with the commissioner of commerce, pursuant to other law, articles of incorporation and articles of amendment shall be filed with the secretary of state.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.151.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.18.0">1981 c 270 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/98/#laws.0.9.0">2009 c 98 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.153">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.153 EFFECTIVE DATE OF ARTICLES.</h1>
        <p>Articles of incorporation are effective and corporate existence begins when the articles of incorporation are filed with the secretary of state accompanied by a payment of $135, which includes a $100 incorporation fee in addition to the $35 filing fee required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.011#stat.302A.011.11">302A.011, subdivision 11</a>. Articles of amendment are effective when filed with the secretary of state or at another time within 30 days after filing if the articles of amendment so provide. Articles of merger must be accompanied by a fee of $60, which includes a $25 merger fee in addition to the $35 filing fee required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.011#stat.302A.011.11">302A.011, subdivision 11</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.153.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.19.0">1981 c 270 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/356/#laws.0.344.0">1981 c 356 s 344</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/404/#laws.0.164.0">1987 c 404 s 164</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.194.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 194</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.15.0">1993 c 17 s 15</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.155">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.155 PRESUMPTION; CERTIFICATE OF INCORPORATION.</h1>
        <p>When the articles of incorporation have been filed with the secretary of state and the required fee has been paid to the secretary of state, it is presumed that all conditions precedent required to be performed by the incorporators have been complied with and that the corporation has been incorporated, and the secretary of state shall issue a certificate of incorporation to the corporation, but this presumption does not apply against this state in a proceeding to cancel or revoke the certificate of incorporation or to compel the involuntary dissolution of the corporation.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.155.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.20.0">1981 c 270 s 20</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">POWERS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.161">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.161 POWERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Generally; limitations.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation has the powers set forth in this section, subject to any limitations provided in any other statute of this state or in its articles.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Duration.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation has perpetual duration.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Legal capacity.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may sue and be sued, complain and defend and participate as a party or otherwise in any legal, administrative, or arbitration proceeding, in its corporate name.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Property ownership.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may purchase, lease, or otherwise acquire, own, hold, improve, use, and otherwise deal in and with, real or personal property, or any interest therein, wherever situated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Property disposition.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may sell, convey, mortgage, create a security interest in, lease, exchange, transfer, or otherwise dispose of all or any part of its real or personal property, or any interest therein, wherever situated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Trading in securities; obligations.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may purchase, subscribe for, or otherwise acquire, own, hold, vote, use, employ, sell, exchange, mortgage, lend, create a security interest in, or otherwise dispose of and otherwise use and deal in and with, securities or other interests in, or obligations of, a person or direct or indirect obligations of any domestic or foreign government or instrumentality thereof.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Contracts; mortgages.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may make contracts and incur liabilities, borrow money, issue its securities, and secure any of its obligations by mortgage of or creation of a security interest in all or any of its property, franchises and income.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Investment.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may invest and reinvest its funds.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Holding property as security.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may take and hold real and personal property, whether or not of a kind sold or otherwise dealt in by the corporation, as security for the payment of money loaned, advanced, or invested.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Location.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may conduct its business, carry on its operations, have offices, and exercise the powers granted by this chapter anywhere in the universe.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Donations.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may make donations, irrespective of corporate benefit, for the public welfare; for social, community, charitable, religious, educational, scientific, civic, literary, and testing for public safety purposes, and for similar or related purposes; for the purpose of fostering national or international amateur sports competition; and for the prevention of cruelty to children and animals.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Pensions; benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may pay pensions, retirement allowances, and compensation for past services to and for the benefit of, and establish, maintain, continue, and carry out, wholly or partially at the expense of the corporation, employee or incentive benefit plans, trusts, and provisions to or for the benefit of, any or all of its and its related organizations' officers, managers, directors, governors, employees, and agents and, in the case of a related organization that is a limited liability company, members who provide services to the limited liability company, and the families, dependents, and beneficiaries of any of them. It may indemnify and purchase and maintain insurance for and on behalf of a fiduciary of any of these employee benefit and incentive plans, trusts, and provisions.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Participating in management.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may participate in any capacity in the promotion, organization, ownership, management, and operation of any organization or in any transaction, undertaking, or arrangement that the participating corporation would have power to conduct by itself, whether or not the participation involves sharing or delegation of control with or to others.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Insurance.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may provide for its benefit life insurance and other insurance with respect to the services of any or all of its officers, directors, employees, and agents, or on the life of a shareholder for the purpose of acquiring at the death of the shareholder any or all shares in the corporation owned by the shareholder.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Corporate seal.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may have, alter at pleasure, and use a corporate seal as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.163">302A.163</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Bylaws.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may adopt, amend, and repeal bylaws relating to the management of the business or the regulation of the affairs of the corporation as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.181">302A.181</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Committees.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may establish committees of the board of directors, elect or appoint persons to the committees, and define their duties as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.241">302A.241</a> and fix their compensation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Officers; employees; agents.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may elect or appoint officers, employees, and agents of the corporation, and define their duties as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.301">302A.301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.361">302A.361</a> and fix their compensation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Securities.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may issue securities and rights to purchase securities as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.401">302A.401</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.425">302A.425</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Loans; guaranties; sureties.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may lend money to, guarantee an obligation of, become a surety for, or otherwise financially assist persons as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.501">302A.501</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Advances.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may make advances to its directors, officers, and employees and those of its subsidiaries as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.505">302A.505</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Indemnification.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation shall indemnify those persons identified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.521">302A.521</a> against certain expenses and liabilities only as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.521">302A.521</a> and may indemnify other persons.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Assumed names.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may conduct all or part of its business under one or more assumed names as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.06">333.06</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.161.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">Other powers.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may have and exercise all other powers necessary or convenient to effect any or all of the business purposes for which the corporation is incorporated.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.161.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.21.0">1981 c 270 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/104/#laws.0.15.0">1987 c 104 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/172/#laws.0.4.0">1989 c 172 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/137/#laws.0.12.0">1993 c 137 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.163">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.163 CORPORATE SEAL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.163.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Seal not required.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may, but need not, have a corporate seal, and the use or nonuse of a corporate seal does not affect the validity, recordability, or enforceability of a document or act. If a corporation has a corporate seal, the use of the seal by the corporation on a document is not necessary.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.163.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required words; use.</span></h2>
          <p>If a corporation has a corporate seal, the seal may consist of a mechanical imprinting device, or a rubber stamp with a facsimile of the seal affixed thereon, or a facsimile or reproduction of either. The seal need include only the word "Seal," but it may also include a part or all of the name of the corporation and a combination, derivation, or abbreviation of either or both of the phrases "a Minnesota Corporation" and "Corporate Seal." If a corporate seal is used, it or a facsimile of it may be affixed, engraved, printed, placed, stamped with indelible ink, or in any other manner reproduced on any document.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.163.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.22.0">1981 c 270 s 22</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.165">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.165 EFFECT OF LACK OF POWER; ULTRA VIRES.</h1>
        <p>The doing, continuing, or performing by a corporation of an act, or an executed or wholly or partially executory contract, conveyance or transfer to or by the corporation, if otherwise lawful, is not invalid because the corporation was without the power to do, continue, or perform the act, contract, conveyance, or transfer, unless the lack of power is established in a court in this state:</p>
        <p>(a) In a proceeding by a shareholder against the corporation to enjoin the doing, continuing, or performing of the act, contract, conveyance, or transfer. If the unauthorized act, continuation, or performance sought to be enjoined is being, or to be, performed or made pursuant to a contract to which the corporation is a party, the court may, if just and reasonable in the circumstances, set aside and enjoin the performance of the contract and in so doing may allow to the corporation or to the other parties to the contract compensation for the loss or damage sustained as a result of the action of the court in setting aside and enjoining the performance of the contract;</p>
        <p>(b) In a proceeding by or in the name of the corporation, whether acting directly or through a legal representative, or through shareholders in a representative or derivative suit, against the incumbent or former officers or directors of the corporation for exceeding or otherwise violating their authority, or against a person having actual knowledge of the lack of power; or</p>
        <p>(c) In a proceeding by the attorney general, as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.757">302A.757</a>, to dissolve the corporation, or in a proceeding by the attorney general to enjoin the corporation from the transaction of unauthorized business.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.165.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.23.0">1981 c 270 s 23</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">ORGANIZATION; BYLAWS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.171">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.171 ORGANIZATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.171.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Role of incorporators.</span></h2>
          <p>If the first board is not named in the articles, the incorporators may elect the first board or may act as directors with all of the powers, rights, duties, and liabilities of directors, until directors are elected or until shares are issued, whichever occurs first.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.171.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Meeting.</span></h2>
          <p>After the filing of articles of incorporation, the incorporators or the directors named in the articles shall either hold an organizational meeting at the call of a majority of the incorporators or of the directors named in the articles, or take written action, for the purposes of transacting business and taking actions necessary or appropriate to complete the organization of the corporation, including, without limitation, amending the articles, electing directors, adopting bylaws, electing officers, adopting banking resolutions, authorizing or ratifying the purchase, lease, or other acquisition of suitable space, furniture, furnishings, supplies, and materials, approving a corporate seal, approving forms of certificates for shares of the corporation, adopting a fiscal year for the corporation, accepting subscriptions for and issuing shares of the corporation, and making any appropriate tax elections. If a meeting is held, the person or persons calling the meeting shall give at least three days' notice of the meeting to each incorporator or director named, stating the date, time, and place of the meeting. Incorporators and directors may waive notice of an organizational meeting in the same manner that a director may waive notice of meetings of the board pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.231#stat.302A.231.5">302A.231, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.171.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.24.0">1981 c 270 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/17/#laws.0.16.0">1993 c 17 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/10/#laws.1.9.0">1997 c 10 art 1 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.181">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.181 BYLAWS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.181.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Generally.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may, but need not, have bylaws. Bylaws may contain any provision relating to the management of the business or the regulation of the affairs of the corporation not inconsistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.201">302A.201</a> or any other provision of law or the articles.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.181.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Power of board.</span></h2>
          <p>Initial bylaws may be adopted pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.171">302A.171</a> by the incorporators or by the first board. Unless reserved by the articles to the shareholders, the power to adopt, amend, or repeal the bylaws is vested in the board. The power of the board is subject to the power of the shareholders, exercisable in the manner provided in subdivision 3, to adopt, amend, or repeal bylaws adopted, amended, or repealed by the board. After the adoption of the initial bylaws, the board shall not adopt, amend, or repeal a bylaw fixing a quorum for meetings of shareholders, prescribing procedures for removing directors or filling vacancies in the board, or fixing the number of directors or their classifications, qualifications, or terms of office, but may adopt or amend a bylaw to increase the number of directors.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.181.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Power of shareholders; procedure.</span></h2>
          <p>If a shareholder or shareholders holding three percent or more of the voting power of the shares entitled to vote propose a resolution for action by the shareholders to adopt, amend, or repeal bylaws adopted, amended, or repealed by the board and the resolution sets forth the provision or provisions proposed for adoption, amendment, or repeal, the limitations and procedures for submitting, considering, and adopting the resolution are the same as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.135">302A.135</a>, subdivisions 2 to 4, for amendment of the articles.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.181.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.25.0">1981 c 270 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.22.0">1982 c 497 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/85/#laws.1.4.0">1999 c 85 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/264/#laws.0.2.0">2000 c 264 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.191">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.191 FORUM SELECTION PROVISIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.191.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Authorization.</span></h2>
          <p>The articles or bylaws may require, consistent with applicable jurisdictional requirements, that any or all internal corporate claims must be brought exclusively in any or all of the courts in this state. The articles or bylaws must not prohibit bringing an internal corporate claim in the courts of this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.191.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>"Internal corporate claim" means any:</p>
          <p>(a) claim that is based upon a violation of a duty under the laws of this state by a current or former director, officer, or shareholder in such capacity;</p>
          <p>(b) derivative action or proceeding brought on behalf of the corporation; or</p>
          <p>(c) action asserting a claim arising under this chapter or the corporation's articles or bylaws.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.191.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/103/#laws.0.4.0">2018 c 103 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">BOARD</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.201">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.201 BOARD.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.201.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Board to manage.</span></h2>
          <p>The business and affairs of a corporation shall be managed by or under the direction of a board, subject to the provisions of subdivision 2 and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.457">302A.457</a>. The members of the first board may be named in the articles or elected by the incorporators pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.171">302A.171</a> or by the shareholders.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.302A.201.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Shareholder management.</span></h2>
          <p>The holders of the shares entitled to vote for directors of the corporation may, by unanimous affirmative vote, take any action that this chapter requires or permits the board to take. As to an action taken by the shareholders in that manner:</p>
          <p>(a) the directors have no duties, liabilities, or responsibilities as directors under this chapter with respect to or arising from the action;</p>
          <p>(b) the shareholders collectively and individually have all of the duties, liabilities, and responsibilities of directors under this chapter with respect to and arising from the action;</p>
          <p>(c) if the action relates to a matter required or permitted by this chapter or by any other law to be approved or adopted by the board, either with or without approval or adoption by the shareholders, the action is deemed to have been approved or adopted by the board; and</p>
          <p>(d) a requirement that an instrument filed with a governmental agency contain a statement that the action has been approved and adopted by the board is satisfied by a statement that the shareholders have taken the action under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.201.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.26.0">1981 c 270 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.23.0">1982 c 497 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/104/#laws.0.16.0">1987 c 104 s 16</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.203">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.203 NUMBER.</h1>
        <p>The board shall consist of one or more directors. The number of directors shall be fixed by or in the manner provided in the articles or bylaws. The number of directors may be increased or, subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.223">302A.223</a>, decreased at any time by amendment to or in the manner provided in the articles or bylaws.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.203.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.27.0">1981 c 270 s 27</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.205">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.205 QUALIFICATIONS; ELECTION.</h1>
        <p>Directors shall be natural persons. The method of election and any additional qualifications for directors may be imposed by or in the manner provided in the articles or bylaws.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.205.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.28.0">1981 c 270 s 28</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.207">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.207 TERMS.</h1>
        <p>Unless fixed terms are provided for in the articles or bylaws, a director serves for an indefinite term that expires at the next regular meeting of the shareholders. A fixed term of a director shall not exceed five years. A director holds office for the term for which the director was elected and until a successor is elected and has qualified, or until the earlier death, resignation, removal, or disqualification of the director.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.207.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.29.0">1981 c 270 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/497/#laws.0.24.0">1982 c 497 s 24</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.209">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.209 ACTS NOT VOID OR VOIDABLE.</h1>
        <p>The expiration of a director's term with or without the election of a qualified successor does not make prior or subsequent acts of the officers or the board void or voidable.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.302A.209.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/270/#laws.0.30.0">1981 c 270 s 30</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.302A.211">
        <h1 class="shn">302A.211 COMPENSATION.</h1>
